Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the activity structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 82. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 107 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the activity decomposition. By minimality, the element a^82 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 107-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For optimality condition pairs (A,B) in activity theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 646 and the invariant I(A,B) = 536 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 646 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the activity structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 536. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
